y=13x-8

B. 4\sqrt{5}
C. 6
D. \frac{\sqrt{65}}{2}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
将点 \((4,8)\) 代入两条抛物线：

对于 \(C_1:y^2=2p_1x\)，有  
\[
8^2=2p_1\cdot 4 \implies 64=8p_1 \implies p_1=8.
\]
抛物线 \(y^2=2p_1x\) 的焦点为 \(\left(\frac{p_1}{2},0\right)\)，所以  
\[

=\sqrt{\frac{65}{4}}
=\frac{\sqrt{65}}{2}.

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

6. 已知函数f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}的最大值为1，则a = （ ）
A. \frac{1}{2}
B. 1
C. \frac{3}{2}
D. 2

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设 \(a\) 为选项中的正数，则 \(e^x+a>0\)。

利用基本不等式

\[
e^x\ge 1+x
\]

且等号当且仅当 \(x=0\) 时成立。

---

### 1. 若 \(a=1\)

\[
f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+1}
\]

因为

\[
e^x+1\ge x+2
\]

所以

\[
\frac{x+2}{e^x+1}\le 1
\]

且当 \(x=0\) 时，

\[
f(0)=\frac{2}{1+1}=1
\]

因此最大值为 \(1\)。

---

### 2. 若 \(a<1\)

则

\[
f(0)=\frac{2}{1+a}>1
\]

最大值不可能为 \(1\)。

---

### 3. 若 \(a>1\)

则

\[
e^x+a>e^x+1\ge x+2
\]

所以对所有 \(x\)，都有

\[
f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}<1
\]

最大值也不可能为 \(1\)。

---

因此只能有

\[
a=1
\]

对应选项 B。

\[
\boxed{B}
\]
Reasoning
We need solve math problem in Chinese. Now analyze fully.

We need solve. f(x)=(x+2)/(e^x+a). Need sup/max equals 1. For positive denominator, f can be negative when x<-2, positive when x>-2, tends 0 as x->infty, tends? as x-> -infty, numerator ~ x (negative), denominator ~ a (if a>0) so -> -infty linear? Actually e^x ->0, denominator->a, numerator-> -∞, so f -> -∞ if a>0. So max finite. Need max=1.

Approach derivative. f'(x) = [(1)(e^x+a) - (x+2)e^x]/(e^x+a)^2 = [e^x+a - (x+2)e^x]/... = [a - x e^x]/(e^x+a)^2. So critical: a = x e^x. For a>0, equation x e^x = a. Since x e^x ranges [-1/e,∞). For a>0, unique x>0? Let's check x e^x for x<0 negative, for x>0 positive increasing from 0 to ∞, so unique positive critical point. Derivative sign: numerator a - x e^x. For x small? at x-> -∞, x e^x ->0-? x negative e^x 0 ->0-, so a - x e^x > a >0, derivative positive. At x ->∞, x e^x ∞, derivative negative. So unique max at x0 where x0 e^{x0}=a. At max, f(x0) = (x0+2)/(e^{x0}+a). Using a=x0 e^{x0}. Denom = e^{x0}(1+x0). Numerator = x0+2. Thus fmax = (x0+2)/(e^{x0}(x0+1)). Set =1. Need solve (x+2)= e^x (x+1). Equivalent e^x = (x+2)/(x+1), x>0? critical positive. Options yield x from a = x e^x. Need find a.

Then e^x(1+x)=(1+x)/x=1+1/x ≈2.762, RHS x+2=2.567, not equal. So no.
a=1/2: x e^x=0.5, x≈0.351734? e^x≈1.4217? left=0.5. Check fmax: denom=e^x(1+x)≈1.4217*1.3517=1.921? numerator=2.3517, ratio=1.224 >1.
a=3/2: x e^x=1.5, x≈0.788? e^x≈2.199? denom=2.199*1.788=3.932, num=2.788 ratio=0.709 <1. So max <1? Wait if fmax as function of a maybe decreasing? For a=1/2 max>1, a=1? >1, a=1.5 <1, root between. Option? a=2? x≈0.852? e^x≈2.345? denom=2.345*1.852=4.343, num=2.852 ratio=0.657. So only maybe 1? But equation exact maybe a=1? Let's verify derivative maybe max condition could include boundary? Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

7. 一百零八塔位于宁夏回族自治区青铜峡市，以其独特的建筑格局和深厚的历史文化闻名遐迩。该塔群共有108座塔，依山势自上而下排成12行，将第i行中塔的座数记为a_i(i=1,2,...,12)，其中a_1=1, a_2=a_3=3, a_4=a_5=5，且a_6, a_7, ..., a_{12}是一个首项为7，公差为2的等差数列。将a_1, a_2, ..., a_{12}分为6组，每组2个数，使得每组的2个数之和可构成一个项数为6且公差为d(d>0)的等差数列，则d = （ ）
A. 2
B. 4
C. 6
D. 8

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由题意可得

\[
a_1=1,\ a_2=a_3=3,\ a_4=a_5=5,
\]

且 \(a_6,a_7,\dots,a_{12}\) 是首项为 \(7\)、公差为 \(2\) 的等差数列，所以

\[
7,9,11,13,15,17,19.
\]

因此 12 个数中各行的塔数为：

\[
1,3,3,5,5,7,9,11,13,15,17,19.
\]

它们的总和为 108。

设分成的 6 组的和构成等差数列，首项为 \(x\)，公差为 \(d\)，则

\[
x+(x+d)+(x+2d)+(x+3d)+(x+4d)+(x+5d)=108.
\]

化简得

\[
6x+15d=108,
\]

即

\[
2x+5d=36.
\]

又因为所有 \(a_i\) 都是奇数，任意两个数相加都是偶数，所以 6 个组之和必须全是偶数。因此这个等差数列的首项 \(x\) 和公差 \(d\) 都应为偶数。

逐项检验选项：

- 若 \(d=2\)，则 \(x=\dfrac{36-10}{2}=13\)，不是偶数，舍去；
- 若 \(d=4\)，则 \(x=\dfrac{36-20}{2}=8\)，可能；
- 若 \(d=6\)，则 \(x=\dfrac{36-30}{2}=3\)，不是偶数，且小于最小可能和，舍去；
- 若 \(d=8\)，则 \(x=\dfrac{36-40}{2}=-2\)，不可能，舍去。

所以只有 \(d=4\) 可能。

验证 \(d=4\) 时可这样分组：

\[
1+7=8,
\]
\[
3+9=12,
\]
\[
3+13=16,
\]
\[
5+15=20,
\]
\[
5+19=24,
\]
\[
11+17=28.
\]

六个和为

\[
8,12,16,20,24,28,
\]

确实是公差为 \(4\) 的等差数列。

因此 \(d=4\)，对应选项 \(\boxed{B}\)。

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

8. 设U={(x_1, x_2, x_3)|x_i \in \{-2,-1,1,2\}, i=1,2,3}\}为空间中64个点构成的集合，点P(1,1,1)，记样本空间\Omega = C_U^P，从\Omega中随机取一个点，定义随机变量X如下：对\Omega中的每个点A(x_1, x_2, x_3)，令X(A) = x_1 + x_2 + x_3，则X的数学期望值为（ ）
A. -\frac{1}{21}
B. -\frac{1}{63}
C. 0
D. \frac{1}{7}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由题意，\(C_U^P\) 表示 \(U\) 中除去点 \(P(1,1,1)\) 的集合，即

\[
\Omega=U\setminus\{P\},
\]

所以 \(\Omega\) 中共有 \(64-1=63\) 个点。

先计算整个集合 \(U\) 中所有点的 \(X=x_1+x_2+x_3\) 的总和。

对任意一个坐标 \(x_i\)，另外两个坐标各有 \(4^2=16\) 种取法，而 \(x_i\) 取 \(-2,-1,1,2\) 各出现 16 次，因此

\[
\sum_{(x_1,x_2,x_3)\in U} x_i
=16(-2-1+1+2)=0.
\]

所以三个坐标分别求和都为 0，故

\[
\sum_{A\in U} X(A)=0.
\]

点 \(P(1,1,1)\) 对应的随机变量值为

\[
X(P)=1+1+1=3.
\]

因此 \(\Omega\) 中所有点的 \(X\) 总和为

\[
0-3=-3.
\]

由于从 \(\Omega\) 中等概率取点，故数学期望为

\[
\mathbb E(X)=\frac{-3}{63}=-\frac{1}{21}.
\]

所以答案是

\[
\boxed{A}
\]

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response

- \(\dfrac{z+3}{z-i}=\dfrac{6+2i}{3+i}=\dfrac{2(3+i)}{3+i}=2\in\mathbb R\)，D 正确。

因此正确选项为：

\]

gaokao_000_009 ✓ BC BC Y Y 18183 51.4 353.6 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

10. 在空间中，A、B为两个定点，动点C到直线AB的距离为2，动点D到直线AB的距离为1。若二面角C - AB - D为60°，则（ ）
A. \angle CAD \ge 60^\circ
B. CD \ge \sqrt{3}
C. 当AB \perp CD时，CD \perp平面ABD
D. 当AB \perp平面ACD时，AC \perp AD

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设直线 \(AB\) 的方向单位向量为 \(\mathbf e\)，并取 \(A\) 为原点。

设 \(C,D\) 在直线 \(AB\) 上的垂足分别为 \(P,Q\)，记

\[
P=p\mathbf e,\qquad Q=q\mathbf e.
\]

再设从垂足指向 \(C,D\) 的单位向量分别为 \(\mathbf u,\mathbf v\)，则

\[
C=P+2\mathbf u=p\mathbf e+2\mathbf u,\qquad 
D=Q+\mathbf v=q\mathbf e+\mathbf v.
\]

其中 \(\mathbf u,\mathbf v\perp \mathbf e\)，且由二面角 \(C-AB-D=60^\circ\) 得

\[
\mathbf u\cdot \mathbf v=\cos 60^\circ=\frac12.
\]

---

### A 选项

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=p\mathbf e+2\mathbf u,\qquad 
\overrightarrow{AD}=q\mathbf e+\mathbf v.
\]

则

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=pq+2(\mathbf u\cdot \mathbf v)
=pq+1.
\]

取 \(p=q=1\)，则

\[
\cos\angle CAD
=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}\sqrt{2}}
=\frac{2}{\sqrt{10}}>\frac12,
\]

所以

\[
\angle CAD<60^\circ.
\]

故 A 错误。

---

### B 选项

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}=(q-p)\mathbf e+\mathbf v-2\mathbf u.
\]

沿 \(AB\) 方向的分量为 \((q-p)\mathbf e\)，垂直于 \(AB\) 的分量为 \(\mathbf v-2\mathbf u\)，因此

\[
CD^2=(q-p)^2+|\mathbf v-2\mathbf u|^2.
\]

而

\[
|\mathbf v-2\mathbf u|^2
=1+4-4(\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v)
=5-4\cdot\frac12=3.
\]

所以

\[
CD^2=(q-p)^2+3\ge 3,
\]

即

\[
CD\ge \sqrt3.
\]

故 B 正确。

---

### C 选项

若 \(AB\perp CD\)，即 \(\overrightarrow{CD}\perp \mathbf e\)，则

\[
(q-p)\mathbf e+\mathbf v-2\mathbf u \perp \mathbf e
\]

得

\[
q-p=0.
\]

于是

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}=\mathbf v-2\mathbf u.
\]

平面 \(ABD\) 由方向 \(\mathbf e\) 和 \(\overrightarrow{AD}=q\mathbf e+\mathbf v\) 张成。计算：

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \mathbf e=0,
\]

且

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=(\mathbf v-2\mathbf u)\cdot(q\mathbf e+\mathbf v)
=1-2\cdot\frac12=0.
\]

因此 \(CD\) 垂直于平面 \(ABD\) 内两条相交方向，又 \(CD\) 过点 \(D\)，故

\[
CD\perp \text{平面 }ABD.
\]

故 C 正确。

---

### D 选项

若 \(AB\perp\) 平面 \(ACD\)，则 \(AB\) 垂直于平面内过 \(A\) 的任意直线，特别地

\[
\mathbf e\perp \overrightarrow{AC},\qquad \mathbf e\perp \overrightarrow{AD}.
\]

于是

\[
p=0,\qquad q=0.
\]

此时

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=2\mathbf u,\qquad 
\overrightarrow{AD}=\mathbf v,
\]

所以

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}\cdot\overrightarrow{AD}
=2(\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v)=1\ne 0.
\]

故 \(AC\) 与 \(AD\) 不垂直，D 错误。

---

综上，正确选项为

\[
\boxed{BC}
\]
Reasoning
We need solve geometry multiple choice. Need reason carefully. Problem Chinese: In space, A, B fixed points, moving point C distance to line AB is 2, moving point D distance to line AB is 1. Dihedral angle C - AB - D is 60°. Then which? Options:
A. angle CAD >= 60°
B. CD >= sqrt3
C. When AB ⟂ CD, CD ⟂ plane ABD
D. When AB ⟂ plane ACD, AC ⟂ AD
Need final answer boxed likely one or multiple? It says 则（ ） with options A-D, maybe multiple-choice? Need determine true statements. We need analyze. Let's set coordinate.

Let AB be x-axis, maybe A at origin, B at (b,0,0) with length L>0 fixed? A,B fixed but unspecified; distances constraints independent? C has distance 2 to AB, D distance 1 to AB. Dihedral angle C-AB-D = 60° means angle between half-planes? Need interpret: Dihedral angle with edge AB, faces containing C and D. Equivalent: take perpendiculars from C and D to line AB, the angle between their projections onto a plane perpendicular to AB equals 60° (or supplementary? gaokao_000_010 ✓ BCD BCD Y Y 25544 56.2 454.7 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

11. 已知圆C_1:(x+1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_2:(x-1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_3:x^2+(y-\sqrt{3})^2=1，直线l:y=kx+b与C_1, C_2, C_3均有两个交点。记l被C_1, C_2, C_3截得的弦长分别为s_1、s_2、s_3，则（ ）
A. k可以取任意实数
B. 满足s_1=s_2=s_3的直线l共有3条
C. 满足s_1+s_2+s_3=3的直线l多于3条
D. 当b=0时，s_1+s_2+s_3的最大值为\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设直线 \(l:y=kx+b\) 写成

\[
kx-y+b=0,
\qquad S=\sqrt{k^2+1}.
\]

三圆半径均为 \(1\)，圆心分别为

\[
O_1(-1,0),\quad O_2(1,0),\quad O_3(0,\sqrt3).
\]

圆心到直线的距离为

\[
d_1=\frac{|b-k|}{S},\qquad
d_2=\frac{|b+k|}{S},\qquad
d_3=\frac{|b-\sqrt3|}{S}.
\]

直线与圆有两个交点当且仅当 \(d_i<1\)。弦长公式为

\[
s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}.
\]

---

### A 判断

取

\[
k=\frac1{\sqrt3},
\qquad S=\sqrt{1+\frac13}=\frac2{\sqrt3}.
\]

此时：

\[
d_1<1\iff \left|b-\frac1{\sqrt3}\right|<\frac2{\sqrt3}
\iff -\frac1{\sqrt3}<b<\sqrt3,
\]

\[
d_2<1\iff \left|b+\frac1{\sqrt3}\right|<\frac2{\sqrt3}
\iff -\sqrt3<b<\frac1{\sqrt3},
\]

所以 \(d_1<1\) 且 \(d_2<1\) 时

\[
-\frac1{\sqrt3}<b<\frac1{\sqrt3}.
\]

而

\[
d_3<1\iff |b-\sqrt3|<\frac2{\sqrt3}
\iff \frac1{\sqrt3}<b<\frac5{\sqrt3}.
\]

两个 \(b\) 的范围无交集，因此当 \(k=\frac1{\sqrt3}\) 时不存在满足题意的直线 \(l\)。

所以 \(k\) 不能取任意实数，**A 错**。

---

### B 判断

三圆半径相同，因此

\[
s_1=s_2=s_3 \iff d_1=d_2=d_3.
\]

先由 \(d_1=d_2\)：

\[
|b-k|=|b+k|
\]

平方得

\[
(b-k)^2=(b+k)^2
\]

\[
4bk=0,
\]

所以

\[
b=0 \quad\text{或}\quad k=0.
\]

#### 情况 1：\(k=0\)

此时

\[
d_1=d_2=|b|,\qquad d_3=|b-\sqrt3|.
\]

令 \(d_1=d_3\)：

\[
|b|=|b-\sqrt3|
\]

得

\[
b=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}.
\]

且

\[
\frac{\sqrt3}{2}<1,
\]

满足两个交点条件。得到一条直线：

\[
y=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}.
\]

#### 情况 2：\(b=0\)

此时

\[
d_1=d_2=\frac{|k|}{S},\qquad d_3=\frac{\sqrt3}{S}.
\]

令 \(d_1=d_3\)：

\[
|k|=\sqrt3
\]

得

\[
k=\pm\sqrt3.
\]

此时

\[
d_1=d_2=d_3=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}<1,
\]

满足条件。得到两条直线：

\[
y=\sqrt3 x,\qquad y=-\sqrt3 x.
\]

综上，满足 \(s_1=s_2=s_3\) 的直线共有 \(3\) 条，**B 对**。

---

### C 判断

由 B 中三条直线可知，它们都有

\[
d_i=\frac{\sqrt3}{2},
\qquad s_i=2\sqrt{1-\frac34}=1,
\]

所以

\[
s_1+s_2+s_3=3.
\]

现在再找其他直线。

取 \(b=0\)，即直线过原点：

\[
l:y=kx.
\]

要满足与 \(C_3\) 有两个交点，需要

\[
d_3=\frac{\sqrt3}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}<1
\iff k^2>2.
\]

此时

\[
s_1=s_2=2\sqrt{1-\frac{k^2}{k^2+1}}
=\frac{2}{\sqrt{k^2+1}},
\]

\[
s_3=2\sqrt{1-\frac{3}{k^2+1}}
=\frac{2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}.
\]

所以

\[
s_1+s_2+s_3
=
\frac{4+2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}.
\]

令其等于 \(3\)：

\[
\frac{4+2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}=3.
\]

设

\[
u=\sqrt{k^2+1},
\qquad u>\sqrt3.
\]

则

\[
4+2\sqrt{u^2-3}=3u.
\]

整理并平方：

\[
2\sqrt{u^2-3}=3u-4,
\]

\[
4(u^2-3)=(3u-4)^2,
\]

\[
5u^2-24u+28=0,
\]

\[
(u-2)(5u-14)=0.
\]

得

\[
u=2 \quad\text{或}\quad u=\frac{14}{5}.
\]

对应

\[
k^2=u^2-1=3 \quad\text{或}\quad k^2=\frac{171}{25}.
\]

其中 \(k^2=3\) 给出 B 中两条直线 \(y=\pm\sqrt3 x\)；而

\[
k^2=\frac{171}{25}
\]

给出两条新直线：

\[
y=\pm\frac{\sqrt{171}}{5}x.
\]

因此满足 \(s_1+s_2+s_3=3\) 的直线至少有

\[
3+2=5
\]

条，多于 \(3\) 条，**C 对**。

---

### D 判断

当 \(b=0\) 时，已得

\[
s_1+s_2+s_3
=
\frac{4+2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}.
\]

令

\[
x=\sqrt{k^2-2}>0,
\]

则

\[
k^2+1=x^2+3,
\]

于是

\[
F(x)=s_1+s_2+s_3
=
\frac{2(x+2)}{\sqrt{x^2+3}}.
\]

求其最大值：

\[
F(x)^2=\frac{4(x+2)^2}{x^2+3}.
\]

我们证明

\[
F(x)^2\le \left(\frac{2\sqrt{21}}3\right)^2=\frac{28}{3}.
\]

等价于

\[
\frac{4(x+2)^2}{x^2+3}\le \frac{28}{3},
\]

即

\[
12(x+2)^2\le 28(x^2+3),
\]

两边除以 \(4\)：

\[
3(x+2)^2\le 7(x^2+3).
\]

展开：

\[
3x^2+12x+12\le 7x^2+21,
\]

\[
0\le 4x^2-12x+9,
\]

\[
0\le (2x-3)^2.
\]

恒成立，等号当

\[
2x-3=0
\iff x=\frac32
\]

时取得。此时

\[
k^2=x^2+2=\frac94+2=\frac{17}{4}>2,
\]

满足条件。

因此最大值为

\[
\frac{2\sqrt{21}}3.
\]

**D 对**。

---

综上，正确选项为

\[
\boxed{BCD}
\]

gaokao_000_011 ✓ \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6} \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6} Y Y 601 62.4 9.6 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

12. 双曲线5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1的离心率为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
将双曲线化为标准形式：

\[
5x^2-6y^2=1 \quad\Longrightarrow\quad \frac{x^2}{1/5}-\frac{y^2}{1/6}=1
\]

所以

\[
a^2=\frac15,\qquad b^2=\frac16
\]

双曲线的离心率

\[
e=\sqrt{1+\frac{b^2}{a^2}}
=\sqrt{1+\frac{1/6}{1/5}}
=\sqrt{1+\frac56}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}
\]

因此答案为

\[
\boxed{\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer math problem in Chinese. Need solve: hyperbola 5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1 eccentricity. Need put final in boxed. Need analyze thoroughly maybe simple.

Equation 5x^2 - 6y^2 =1. Standard: x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6)=1. So a^2 = 1/5, b^2 = 1/6. Hyperbola horizontal transverse axis. c^2 = a^2 + b^2 = 1/5 + 1/6 = 6/30+5/30=11/30. e = c/a = sqrt(c^2/a^2) = sqrt((11/30)/(1/5)) = sqrt(11/30*5) = sqrt(55/30)=sqrt(11/6). So e = sqrt(11/6) maybe rationalize sqrt66/6? sqrt(11/6) = sqrt66/6? Since sqrt(11/6) = sqrt66 / 6? Multiply numerator denominator by 6: sqrt(66)/6? Check: sqrt(11/6)=sqrt(66/36)=sqrt66/6. Yes. Which form? Usually eccentricity >1: sqrt(11/6) ≈1.354. Could be \sqrt{\frac{11}{6}} or \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}. Need boxed.

Need ensure no mistake: For hyperbola x^2/a^2 - y^2/b^2 =1, e = sqrt(1 + b^2/a^2). b^2/a^2 = (1/6)/(1/5)=5/6. 1+5/6=11/6. Good.

Need final: brief step-by-step in Chinese. Put final in boxed. Could include equation. Ensure answer in \boxed{}.
Grader reason
The eccentricity is correctly computed as sqrt(11/6), which is equivalent to sqrt(66)/6, and the final answer is placed in \boxed{} as required.

gaokao_000_012 ✓ \frac{3\pi}{2}, 1 \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\ f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1 Y Y 10913 49.2 221.7 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

13. 已知f(x)=2sin(ax+\theta) (a \in Z, 0 \le \theta < 2\pi)是偶函数，f(x)在区间(0, \frac{\pi}{2})单调递增。则\theta =__________，f(\frac{2\pi}{3}) =__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由偶函数条件：

\[
f(-x)=f(x)
\]

得

\[
2\sin(\theta-ax)=2\sin(\theta+ax)
\]

即

\[
\sin(\theta-ax)-\sin(\theta+ax)=0
\]

利用差化积：

\[
-2\cos\theta\sin(ax)=0
\]

对所有 \(x\) 成立。若 \(a=0\)，则 \(f\) 为常函数，不符合通常“单调递增”（严格递增）的要求，所以 \(a\neq 0\)。因此必须有

\[
\cos\theta=0
\]

所以

\[
\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\quad \text{或}\quad \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

令 \(n=|a|\in \mathbb N^*\)。

若 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)，则

\[
f(x)=2\cos(nx)
\]

\[
f'(x)=-2n\sin(nx)
\]

在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 左端附近 \(\sin(nx)>0\)，故 \(f'(x)<0\)，不是递增，舍去。

若 \(\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}\)，则

\[
f(x)=-2\cos(nx)
\]

\[
f'(x)=2n\sin(nx)
\]

要在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 上递增，需要 \(\sin(nx)\ge 0\) 恒成立。由于 \(n\) 为整数，只有

\[
n=1 \quad \text{或} \quad n=2
\]

满足，即 \(|a|=1\) 或 \(2\)。因此

\[
\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

此时

\[
f(x)=-2\cos(ax)
\]

所以

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)
=-2\cos\left(\frac{2\pi a}{3}\right)
\]

当 \(|a|=1\) 时，

\[
\cos\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-\frac12
\]

当 \(|a|=2\) 时，

\[
\cos\left(\frac{4\pi}{3}\right)=-\frac12
\]

因此都有

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1
\]

\[
\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\ f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer math problem in Chinese. Need solve carefully.

Problem: 13. Given f(x)=2 sin(a x + θ), a ∈ Z, 0 ≤ θ < 2π is even function, f(x) is monotonically increasing on interval (0, π/2). Then θ = ______, f(2π/3)=______.

Need final in boxed.

We need reason. f even: f(-x)=f(x) for all x. f(x)=2 sin(a x + θ). For all x: sin(-a x + θ)=sin(a x + θ). Difference? Use identity sin u - sin v = 2 cos((u+v)/2) sin((u-v)/2)=0. Here u=θ-a x, v=θ+a x: sin(θ-ax)-sin(θ+ax)= -2 cos θ sin(ax) =0 for all x. So cos θ sin(ax)=0 ∀x. If a ≠ 0, sin(ax) not identically zero for x all real, so cos θ=0. If a=0, f=2 sin θ constant even for any θ. Need also increasing on (0,π/2). If a=0 constant not increasing? Usually monotone increasing (strict?) In Chinese 单调递增 often nondecreasing? A constant function is both nondecreasing and nonincreasing, but many high school contexts 单调递增 maybe strictly increasing? Need infer. If a=0, f constant, not usually considered monotone increasing? It can be. But then θ not unique? Need determine unique answer, so likely a ≠0 and cos θ=0. θ = π/2 or 3π/2.

Then f(x)=2 sin(a x + π/2)=2 cos(a x) or θ=3π/2 gives 2 sin(ax+3π/2)= -2 cos(ax). Need monotone increasing on (0,π/2). Need find integer a satisfying monotone increasing and determine θ, f(2π/3). But a not given? We need deduce? Let's analyze.

For θ=π/2: f=2 cos(a x). On (0,π/2), derivative -2a sin(ax). Need nondecreasing/strict increasing? For integer a.

For θ=3π/2: f=-2 cos(ax). derivative 2a sin(ax). Need sign.

If monotone increasing on interval, for f=2 cos(ax): Need derivative ≥0 or >? derivative -2a sin(ax) ≥0 for x∈(0,π/2) if nondecreasing? But if a integer nonzero, sin(ax) changes sign for |a|>1 because ax over interval length |a|π/2. Unless interval length less than period? Need examine.

If |a|≥2, ax range length ≥π, sin changes sign multiple times. So derivative sign changes; not monotone? But function might still be monotone if no extrema? cos on interval with multiple oscillations not monotone. For monotonic on interval, need derivative not change sign (if differentiable) and no local extrema. For a≥? likely |a|=1 maybe. But let's verify.

For θ=π/2, a positive? f=2 cos(ax). If a=1, derivative=-2 sin x <0 on (0,π/2): decreasing, not increasing. If a=-1: cos(-x)=cos x same, decreasing. If |a|=1 not increasing.

For θ=3π/2, f=-2 cos(ax). If a=1, f=-2 cos x derivative=2 sin x>0 on (0,π/2), increasing. If a=-1, same. So a=±1, θ=3π/2 seems.

But need consider if a=0: constant not strictly increasing. If nondecreasing, any θ? Wait if a=0, f=2 sin θ constant, even, nondecreasing; then θ not unique and f(2π/3) not unique. Problem expects unique, so increasing means strict or they assume a? maybe a ∈ Z not zero? It doesn't state a ≠0. But condition monotone increasing likely strict in Chinese high school? Usually “单调递增” means for x1<x2, f(x1)<f(x2); “单调不减” is nondecreasing. So constant not allowed. Good.

But wait could there be other a where f monotone increasing without derivative sign constant? Let's rigorously examine.

Even condition gave cosθ=0 if a≠0. Thus f=±2 cos(ax) with sign ± depending on θ. Need f increasing on (0,π/2). Let's examine integer a.

Let f=ε 2 cos(ax), ε=+1 (θ=π/2) or -1 (θ=3π/2), a∈Z\{0}. Since cos is even, sign of a irrelevant: cos(ax)=cos(|a|x). So set n=|a|≥1 integer. f=2ε cos(nx). Need strictly increasing on (0,π/2).

Derivative f'= -2ε n sin(nx). For strict monotone increasing, derivative may be ≥0? But derivative can vanish at endpoints and maybe inside if sin(nx)=0. If derivative positive except zeros okay. Need sign of -ε n sin(nx) nonnegative on (0,π/2). Since sin(nx) changes sign as nx crosses multiples of π. For n≥2, interval (0, nπ/2). If n≥2, includes at least π? For n=2: interval (0,π) sin positive on (0,π), derivative sign constant if -ε positive? Wait n=2: sin(2x)>0 for x∈(0,π/2). Derivative sign constant! So f could be monotone. But does cos(2x) monotone on (0,π/2)? cos 2x decreases from 1 to -1, strictly decreasing. -2 cos 2x increases from -1 to 1. Indeed -2 cos(2x) is increasing on (0,π/2). That's a possibility: a=2, θ=3π/2. Then f(2π/3)= -2 cos(4π/3)= -2(-1/2)=1. For a=1 θ=3π/2 f(2π/3)= -2 cos(2π/3)= -2(-1/2)=1 as well. For n=3: cos(3x) on (0,π/2): from 1 to cos 3π/2=0, but oscillates: derivative -3 sin 3x changes sign at π/3 (x=π/9? wait 3x=π -> x=π/3) and at? interval length 3π/2 includes one full oscillation? sin 3x positive (0,π/3), negative (π/3,2π/3) but 2π/3 > π/2? interval ends at π/2, 3x=3π/2. So derivative sign changes: not monotone. For n=4: sin 4x positive on (0,π/4), negative on (π/4,π/2) changes; not monotone. In general if n even? Let's analyze. n=2k. On (0,π/2), nx∈(0,kπ). sin(nx) alternates sign k times. For k=1 (n=2), only positive. For k≥2 (n≥4), alternates. So not monotone derivative sign.
```
由偶函数：
f(-x)=f(x) => sin(θ-ax)=sin(θ+ax)
=> 2cosθ sin(ax)=0 对所有x成立。
若 a=0，则 f 为常数，不严格单调递增，故 a≠0，于是 cosθ=0，θ=π/2 或 3π/2。

令 n=|a|∈N^*。
- 若 θ=π/2，f=2cos(nx)，f'=-2n sin(nx)。在 (0,π/2) 左端附近 sin(nx)>0，f'<0，不可能递增。
- 若 θ=3π/2，f=-2cos(nx)，f'=2n sin(nx)。要递增，需 sin(nx)≥0 在 (0,π/2) 上恒成立。n为整数：n=1,2 时 sin(nx)>0；n≥3 时存在 x 使 nx∈(π,2π)，sin(nx)<0，矛盾。因此 n=1或2，且 θ=3π/2。

于是 f(x)=-2cos(ax)，且 |a|=1或2。
f(2π/3)=-2cos(2πa/3)。
当 |a|=1：cos(±2π/3)=-1/2；当 |a|=2：cos(±4π/3)=-1/2。
故 f(2π/3)=1。

\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\ f(\frac{2\pi}{3})=1}
```

gaokao_000_013 ✓ \sqrt[3]{\frac{3}{2}} \sqrt[3]{\frac32} Y Y 28447 60.7 468.5 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

14. 设实数q满足：存在数列{a_n}，使得对于任意n \in N^*，均有a_1 + a_2 + \cdots + a_{3n} = n^2 + n，且{a_n}中有某连续9项a_k, a_{k+1}, \cdots, a_{k+8}是公比为q的等比数列。则q的最大值为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
解：先求每个“3元块”的和。对任意 \(m\in\mathbb N^*\)，有

\[
a_{3m-2}+a_{3m-1}+a_{3m}
=(m^2+m)-\big((m-1)^2+(m-1)\big)=2m.
\]

即第 \(m\) 个 3 元块的和为 \(2m\)。

设连续 9 项为

\[
x_1,x_2,\dots,x_9,\qquad x_i=Aq^{i-1}\quad (i=1,\dots,9).
\]

由于这 9 项中至少含有完整的 3 元块，而完整块和为正，所以相关项不全为 \(0\)。又

\[
1+q+q^2>0,
\]

因此任意连续 3 项之和可写成

\[
T_i=x_i+x_{i+1}+x_{i+2}
=Aq^{i-1}(1+q+q^2),\qquad i=1,\dots,7.
\]

按 \(k\) 在 3 元块中的位置分类讨论。

---

### 1. \(k\) 是某 3 元块的第一项

设 \(k=3m-2\)。此时 9 项恰好覆盖第 \(m,m+1,m+2\) 个完整块，故

\[
T_1=2m,\qquad T_4=2m+2,\qquad T_7=2m+4.
\]

于是

\[
q^3=\frac{T_4}{T_1}=\frac{m+1}{m},
\qquad
q^6=\frac{T_7}{T_1}=\frac{m+2}{m}.
\]

但 \(q^6=(q^3)^2\)，所以应有

\[
\frac{m+2}{m}=\left(\frac{m+1}{m}\right)^2,
\]

即

\[
m(m+2)=(m+1)^2,
\]

这不可能成立。因此此种情形不存在。

---

### 2. \(k\) 是某 3 元块的第二项

设 \(k=3m-1\)。此时完整块为第 \(m+1,m+2\) 块，对应

\[
T_3=2m+2,\qquad T_6=2m+4.
\]

因此

\[
q^3=\frac{T_6}{T_3}
=\frac{2m+4}{2m+2}
=\frac{m+2}{m+1}.
\]

由于 \(m\ge1\)，有

\[
q^3=\frac{m+2}{m+1}\le \frac32.
\]

---

### 3. \(k\) 是某 3 元块的第三项

设 \(k=3m\)。此时完整块仍为第 \(m+1,m+2\) 块，对应

\[
T_2=2m+2,\qquad T_5=2m+4.
\]

同理，

\[
q^3=\frac{T_5}{T_2}
=\frac{m+2}{m+1}
\le \frac32.
\]

---

综上，任何满足条件的 \(q\) 都有

\[
q^3\le \frac32,
\]

且由上述比值可知 \(q>0\)，所以

\[
q\le \sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]

下面说明等号可以取到。

令

\[
q_0=\sqrt[3]{\frac32},\qquad F=1+q_0+q_0^2,
\]

取

\[
A=\frac{4}{q_0^2F},
\]

并定义

\[
a_{2+i}=Aq_0^i\quad (i=0,1,\dots,8).
\]

则

\[
a_4+a_5+a_6=Aq_0^2F=4,
\]

\[
a_7+a_8+a_9=Aq_0^5F=Aq_0^2F\cdot q_0^3=4\cdot \frac32=6.
\]

再补充其余项，使每个 3 元块和为 \(2m\)，例如：

\[
a_1=2-a_2-a_3,
\]

\[
a_{11}=8-a_{10},\qquad a_{12}=0,
\]

并对 \(r\ge5\) 令

\[
a_{3r-2}=2r,\qquad a_{3r-1}=a_{3r}=0.
\]

于是每个 3 元块的和依次为

\[
2,4,6,8,10,\dots
\]

因此

\[
a_1+\cdots+a_{3n}=\sum_{m=1}^n 2m=n^2+n.
\]

而 \(a_2,a_3,\dots,a_{10}\) 是公比为 \(q_0\) 的等比数列。

所以 \(q\) 的最大值为

\[
\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac32}}
\]

解：
由条件，记第m个“3元块”的和为
B_m=a_{3m-2}+a_{3m-1}+a_{3m}.
则
B_m=(m^2+m)-((m-1)^2+(m-1))=2m (m≥1).
于是问题转化为：在满足各3元块和为2,4,6,...的数列中，取连续9项成等比，求公比最大值。

设这9项为 x_1,...,x_9，且 x_i=A q^{i-1}. 因其中含有和为正数的完整3元块，故 A≠0；又 1+q+q^2>0。任意连续3项和为
T_i=x_i+x_{i+1}+x_{i+2}=A q^{i-1}(1+q+q^2), i=1,...,7.

按k所在位置分三类。

(1) k为某3元块首项：k=3m-2。此时三个完整块对应 T_1,T_4,T_7：
T_1=2m, T_4=2m+2, T_7=2m+4.
故 q^3=T_4/T_1=(m+1)/m, q^6=T_7/T_1=(m+2)/m. 但 q^6=(q^3)^2，得 (m+1)^2=m(m+2)，矛盾。故此情形不存在。

(2) k为某3元块第二项：k=3m-1。完整块为第m+1、m+2块，对应 T_3,T_6：
T_3=2m+2, T_6=2m+4.
故 q^3=T_6/T_3=(m+2)/(m+1)≤3/2.

(3) k为某3元块第三项：k=3m。完整块为第m+1、m+2块，对应 T_2,T_5：
T_2=2m+2, T_5=2m+4.
同样 q^3=(m+2)/(m+1)≤3/2.

因此在任何可行情形均有 q^3≤3/2，且 q^3>0，所以 q≤∛(3/2).

下面证明等号可取。令 q0=∛(3/2)，F=1+q0+q0^2，取 A=4/(q0^2 F)。定义

a_4+a_5+a_6=A q0^2 F=4,
a_7+a_8+a_9=A q0^5 F=4q0^3=6.
再令
a_1=2-a_2-a_3,
a_{11}=8-a_{10}, a_{12}=0,

---

解：
由条件，对任意 m≥1，
\[
a_{3m-2}+a_{3m-1}+a_{3m} = (m^2+m)-((m-1)^2+(m-1))=2m.
\]
称这三个项为第 m 个 3 元块。

设连续9项为 \(x_1,\dots,x_9\)，且 \(x_i=Aq^{i-1}\) (i=1,...,9). 若 A=0，则9项全0，与其中完整3元块和为正矛盾，故 A≠0. 又 \(1+q+q^2>0\). 记
\[
T_i=x_i+x_{i+1}+x_{i+2}=Aq^{i-1}(1+q+q^2),\quad i=1,...,7.
\]

Then cases...

---

Let's be precise in case1: If k is first term of a block, let k=3m-2. Then the 9 terms are exactly three complete blocks: block m, m+1, m+2. Thus T_1=2m, T_4=2m+2, T_7=2m+4. Therefore
\[
q^3=\frac{T_4}{T_1}=\frac{m+1}{m},\quad q^6=\frac{T_7}{T_1}=\frac{m+2}{m}.
\]
But \(q^6=(q^3)^2\), so
\[
\frac{m+2}{m}=\left(\frac{m+1}{m}\right)^2 \implies m(m+2)=(m+1)^2,
\]
impossible. Thus this case cannot occur.

Case2: If k=3m-1, complete blocks are m+1 and m+2, corresponding to T_3 and T_6. Then
\[
q^3=\frac{T_6}{T_3}=\frac{2m+4}{2m+2}=\frac{m+2}{m+1}\le \frac32.
\]
Case3: If k=3m, complete blocks m+1,m+2 correspond to T_2,T_5. Same equation.

Hence for any possible q, \(q^3\le3/2\), so \(q\le\sqrt[3]{3/2}\).

Representation x_i=A q^{i-1} if A=0 all zero. But if A≠0, q=0 gives x_1 nonzero, x_i=0 for i≥2. In case1 T_4=0? Actually T_4=x4+x5+x6=0, but target positive, impossible. In case2 T_3=0? target positive, impossible. So division by T_3/T_6 not issue because they are positive. Good.

Now, let's maybe phrase: "由于这9项中含有完整3元块，其和为正，故所涉及的 T_i 均非零，可作商。" Good.

Now, let's think if there is any issue with taking cube root of both sides if q^3≤3/2 but q could be negative? Cube root monotonic, so q≤∛(3/2). But we also have q^3 positive in cases, so q positive. Good.

Now, let's maybe include "等号在 m=1 时取得" in upper bound. Then construction shows.

Now, final answer ready.
